Zombindreas

The whole GTA San Andreas map is now zombified! This is version 1. This was underdevelopment since 3 days and now its finally done. Its conversion took me 20 minutes because ConvertFFS doesn't support much objects at a time. I'll keep updating this with more and more objects all over San Andreas

Version 1
*Released*

Version 2
*Cancelled due to too many MORE objects there were decided to be added*
